P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-AA — Controlled Activity Intake Production Auth Mapping Design Gate
Created: 25 May 2026, Europe/Warsaw

Status
PRODUCTION AUTH MAPPING DESIGN GATE CREATED / DOC ONLY / NO ROUTE CHANGE / NO DATABASE WRITE / NO SQL EXECUTION / NO AI CALL / NO ACTIVITY CREATED

Baseline
- Previous block: P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-Z
- Previous commit: 487f444
- Previous message: docs: verify C8-I-D4-L-L-O-Z route auth integration

Gate decision
PASS_PRODUCTION_AUTH_MAPPING_CONTRACT_DESIGN_ONLY

Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-AB - Controlled Activity Intake Production Auth Mapping Contract Design

Reason for O-AA
The controlled activity intake route now uses authContext.ts for no-write preview mode.
The route is still protected by explicit no-write preview headers and does not perform any database writes.
Before production write mode or Supabase insert can be considered, the project needs a production auth mapping contract:
server-side authenticated identity -> stable appUserId -> optional actor/organization/space context.

Current route state
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts exists.
- Route is verified by O-Z.
- Route imports buildControlledActivityIntakeNoWritePreviewTrustedContext.
- Route uses isControlledActivityIntakeRouteAuthContextFailure.
- Route is still no_write_preview only.
- Route still requires x-controlled-intake-no-write-preview: true.
- Route still requires x-controlled-intake-preview-app-user-id only for preview mode.
- Route does not import Supabase.
- Route does not call createClient.
- Route does not call .from().
- Route does not call .insert().
- Route does not call AI.
- Route does not persist category candidates, Value Objects, state facts, state deltas, or snapshots.

Current authContext.ts state
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/authContext.ts exists.
- It provides buildControlledActivityIntakeTrustedContextFromAuth.
- It provides buildControlledActivityIntakeNoWritePreviewTrustedContext.
- It provides assertControlledActivityIntakeTrustedContext.
- It requires server-side context.
- It rejects client ownership.
- It marks preview headers as not accepted for production write.
- It does not execute database reads or writes.
- It does not execute SQL.
- It does not call AI.

Production auth mapping target
The future production mapping must:
1. Read authenticated identity only from server-side session/Auth0 context.
2. Never accept user_id, appUserId, actorId, organizationId, or spaceId from request body.
3. Resolve stable appUserId from server-side identity mapping.
4. Resolve actorId/organizationId/spaceId only from server-verified context.
5. Fail closed if session identity is missing.
6. Fail closed if appUserId mapping is missing.
7. Keep preview mode separate from production mode.
8. Keep DB write disabled until a later explicit write gate.
9. Keep AI/category/state processing disabled until later semantic gates.

Allowed after O-AA only if gate passes
- Create a production auth mapping contract/design document.
- Create no-write-only helper contracts if needed.
- Inspect existing Auth0/session/user-sync code.
- Define exact source of appUserId mapping.
- Define failure codes and route response boundaries.
- Continue avoiding database writes.

Forbidden after O-AA
- Do not execute DB writes.
- Do not execute SQL.
- Do not add Supabase insert.
- Do not convert preview route into production write route.
- Do not trust client-provided ownership fields.
- Do not call AI.
- Do not run Category Derivation.
- Do not persist category candidates.
- Do not create Value Objects.
- Do not write state facts, deltas, or snapshots.

- Auth evidence count: 660
- Mapping evidence count: 600

Important limitation

It does not implement production Auth0/session integration.

It does not approve Supabase insert.
It does not approve a production write route.
It does not approve AI or semantic persistence.

- Do not execute SQL.
- Do not call AI.
